- **stage-ordering**: Every stage is a set of greedy regexes over the whole text. There is no arbitration: whichever stage reaches a span of characters first claims it, and the stage that would have matched a *longer, more specific* span gets whatever debris is left. ([steve-piece/bytheslice](https://github.com/steve-piece/bytheslice/blob/HEAD/skills/run-the-day/agents/stage-runner.md))

- **stage-session**: A lifecycle stage session dispatched by the iteration lead (lifecycle-kit/templates/lead.md). Runs exactly one stage skill to completion — the entry stamp, commits, every state write happen here — and batches decision-shaped escalations back to the lead instead of pausing for the user directly. Use this type when a live lead dispatches a stage; a stage run with no live lead is an ordinary skill invocation and needs no custom type. ([checkwright/checkwright](https://github.com/checkwright/checkwright/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stage-session.md))

- **stage-standard**: Carry out one lifecycle stage by following the skill that owns it. - **stage-tester**: Тестировщик одного этапа задачи в цикле этапа (docs/stage-cycle.md) - после исполнителя пишет тесты YAxUnit этапа по спеке и брифу, проверяет их диагностикой, гоняет в рабочей базе и отчитывается по формату. Код этапа не правит, не коммитит. Участвует, только если в проекте подключён YAxUnit и у этапа есть тесты. Запускается куратором из скилла stage, а не напрямую. - **state-cache-data**: Fluidez de dados no client: optimistic UI, SWR/React Query, cache invalidation correta, offline-first com Service Worker, conflict resolution (last-writer-wins é ruim — use CRDT ou versioning), sync engine, suspense + streaming. Aplicação parece instantânea mesmo em conexão ruim. ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/state-cache-data.md))
- **state-capture-auditor**: Audits ImWeb parameter changes for the group/Display-State-capture decision — the bug class that fails silently on reload, on another machine, or on another origin. Use after adding or changing any parameter, before shipping a phase. ([imweb-project/ImWeb](https://github.com/imweb-project/ImWeb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/state-capture-auditor.md) · ★12)
- **state-checkpoint**: Goal: survive usage timeouts and multi-tool parallel work by keeping durable, compact state files outside the chat. ([AKSoftCode/aicrew](https://github.com/AKSoftCode/aicrew/blob/HEAD/skills/agents/state-checkpoint.md) · ★3)
- **state-claims**: Check present-tense claims in the documentation against the running fleet, and report the ones measurement contradicts. Use for a documentation-versus-reality audit, or when a document's description of current state is in doubt. ([NicolasPogorzelski/homelab-server-architecture](https://github.com/NicolasPogorzelski/homelab-server-architecture/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/state-claims.md) · ★2)
- **state-clerk**: Use `ws decision new` / `ws lesson new` where they fit. Every decision records who asked, why, and what was rejected. The cheap tier is unvalidated until the model-routing experiment passes. ([CS3227-2610-MP4-Easel/CS3227-2610-MP4](https://github.com/CS3227-2610-MP4-Easel/CS3227-2610-MP4/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/project-state/templates/agents/state-clerk.md) · 5 copies)
- **state-clerk**: Make residual state edits scripts cannot (decision and deviation text) in the branch state. ([CS3227-2610-MP4-Easel/CS3227-2610-MP4](https://github.com/CS3227-2610-MP4-Easel/CS3227-2610-MP4/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/state-clerk.md))
